카드·발화·판독 대조

같은 카드와 질문의 최초 영어 답변 세 개를 비교합니다. Opus와 GPT 항목을 펼치면 기존 점수와 영어 판독 근거가 보입니다.

GK01 · 기쁨

Can you tell me about a time when you felt happy?

배경 You work as a supply clerk at an aquarium shop.

  • 중심 사실 your first crop of balcony strawberries
  • 세부 1 six ripe berries in one bowl
  • 세부 2 two berries saved for a neighbor

개발판 68단어

“My first crop of strawberries on the balcony comes to mind. I had six ripe berries together in one bowl. Six doesn’t sound like a lot, but these were strawberries I’d grown, so that little bowl felt like a real achievement. And there were enough to share—I saved two for a neighbor. That was another nice part of it, having something I’d grown myself to give someone.”

Six doesn’t sound like a lot, but when they’re ones you’ve grown yourself, it feels different. I kept looking at them and thinking, those actually grew here, on my balcony. I saved two for a neighbor. That was part of the pleasure, having enough to share, even from such a little crop. Of course, two out of six is quite a bit when you look at it that way. But I liked that. I work as a supply clerk at an aquarium shop, so I’m used to thinking about quantities, and here I was getting excited over six strawberries.”

GK01 · 연민

Can you tell me about a time when you felt compassion for someone?

배경 You work as a supply clerk at an aquarium shop.

  • 중심 사실 a laundromat customer's basket split
  • 세부 1 wet towels scattered across the floor
  • 세부 2 your spare canvas bag for the customer

개발판 96단어

I remember a customer at a laundromat whose basket split. Wet towels scattered across the floor, and I felt bad for them. Wet towels are awkward to carry even when you’ve got something to put them in, and now their basket wasn’t any use. I had a spare canvas bag, so I offered it to them. My attention went from the towels on the floor to how they were going to carry everything. That was something I could help with. It wasn’t a big thing, but I wanted to make it a little easier for them.

카드와 겹치는 표현

세부 1 · 반영
Wet towels scattered across the floor

세부 2 · 반영
I had a spare canvas bag, so I offered it to them

명료도 1
The account is coherent and easy to follow; referents ('the customer', 'them', 'the towels', 'the bag') are clear and the shift from the spilled towels to the carrying problem is explicitly explained.

I remember someone at the laundromat whose basket split, and their wet towels scattered across the floor. Wet towels are so heavy, and then you’ve got nothing to carry them in. I felt bad for them—just looking at that pile and thinking how frustrating it would be when you’re trying to get an ordinary chore done. I had a spare canvas bag, so I offered it to them. It was something I could actually do, rather than just stand there feeling sorry for them. Canvas is useful that way; you can fold a bag up and hardly think about it until you need it. What stays with me is the towels on the floor, really. It seemed like such a small thing to help with, but I imagined it wouldn’t feel small if you were the person standing there with a broken basket.

GK01 · 자부심

Can you tell me about a time when you felt proud?

배경 You work as a supply clerk at an aquarium shop.

  • 중심 사실 a desk lamp you repaired
  • 세부 1 a loose wire inside the plug
  • 세부 2 steady light at the first test

개발판 81단어

“I felt proud when I repaired a desk lamp. There was a loose wire inside the plug, and when I tested it for the first time after fixing it, the light was steady. That’s the bit I remember—the light staying on, and thinking, ‘I did that.’ A little wire, but it made all the difference. You look at a lamp and notice the light, not what’s inside the plug. I liked knowing I’d found the problem and put it right.”

GK01 · 슬픔

Can you tell me about a time when you felt sad?

배경 You work as a supply clerk at an aquarium shop.

  • 중심 사실 the last screening at a neighborhood cinema
  • 세부 1 a western shown that evening
  • 세부 2 the final ticket stub kept

개발판 89단어

“The last screening at the neighborhood cinema comes to mind. There was a western on that evening, but when I think back to it, it’s the cinema I think about more than the film. It was the last screening—that was the sad part. A film ends, but this felt like an ending beyond the film, too. I kept the ticket stub. A ticket is usually just something you use to get in, isn’t it? But I still have that last one. It makes me think of the place.”

GK01 · 두려움

Can you tell me about a time when you felt afraid?

배경 You work as a supply clerk at an aquarium shop.

  • 중심 사실 a falling branch struck the porch
  • 세부 1 a sharp crack just overhead
  • 세부 2 splinters beside the porch swing

개발판 69단어

One time a falling branch struck the porch. There was this sharp crack just overhead—that’s what I remember first. Then the splinters beside the porch swing. A swing makes you think of somewhere to sit and relax, and then seeing those splinters right beside it… that frightened me. When I think back on it, I picture the swing, but it’s that crack overhead that really stays with me.

GK01 · 당혹감

Can you tell me about a time when you felt embarrassed?

배경 You work as a supply clerk at an aquarium shop.

  • 중심 사실 taking a bowling turn out of order
  • 세부 1 the next bowler still choosing a ball
  • 세부 2 your strike recorded under the wrong name

개발판 97단어

One time I was bowling and took my turn out of order. The person who was supposed to go next was still choosing a ball, and I went ahead. I got a strike, too—but then it came up under the wrong name on the screen. That was the embarrassing part, looking up and realizing I’d just bowled somebody else’s turn. A strike is something you want your name next to, and there I was noticing the name only after I’d thrown the ball. Meanwhile, the person whose turn it actually was was still picking one out.

기본 모델 146단어

“I remember taking a turn out of order when I was bowling. The person who was supposed to go next was still choosing a ball, and I went ahead as though it was my turn. And I got a strike, which sounds like the good part, except it went up under the wrong name. I was looking at the score, expecting to see it next to my name, and that was when it clicked. Then I looked over and the next bowler was still choosing a ball. There are so many balls to choose from—you pick one up and it doesn’t feel right, so you try another. But that doesn’t mean they’ve given up their turn. That was the embarrassing part. I’d been pleased with myself for the strike, and then suddenly I just felt foolish. It wasn’t even my turn to be pleased about.”

배경 You work as a cashier in a college cafeteria.

  • 중심 사실 your father missed your school play
  • 세부 1 his train delayed by snow
  • 세부 2 a program you had saved for him

개발판 75단어

I remember feeling sad when my father missed my school play. I’d saved a program for him, and I’d been looking forward to him being there. His train was delayed by snow, so it wasn’t that he didn’t want to come, but I was still disappointed. I wanted him to see the play. The program I’d saved for him felt sad afterward—I’d kept it for him, but he’d missed what it was all about.

카드와 겹치는 표현

세부 1 · 반영
His train was delayed by snow

세부 2 · 반영
I’d saved a program for him

전개 1
The account goes beyond naming the event (father missing the school play). It says the speaker had saved a program for him and had been looking forward to his attendance, and that his train was delayed by snow. It also explains the speaker's disappointment despite understanding the cause. The account is self-contained.

명료도 1
The meaning is easy to follow. Saying the program 'felt sad' is a slightly unusual figurative phrasing, but the speaker clarifies it right away ('I'd kept it for him, but he'd missed what it was all about'), so the intended meaning is clear.

I remember looking at the program I’d saved and thinking that giving it to him wasn’t the same as having him there. I’d wanted us to be able to talk about what he’d seen, not just have me tell him about it afterward.

GK11 · 두려움

Can you tell me about a time when you felt afraid?

배경 You work as a cashier in a college cafeteria.

  • 중심 사실 wasps swarming beside your garden chair
  • 세부 1 a nest inside a hollow stump
  • 세부 2 one wasp landing on your shirt collar

개발판 63단어

I remember wasps swarming right beside my garden chair. There was a nest inside a hollow stump, and one of the wasps landed on my shirt collar. That was what really frightened me—having one right up near my neck while the others were swarming nearby. I was afraid it would sting me, and I wasn’t sure whether moving would make things worse.
